Result: 基于CXR-CLIP检查点，该方法在双病理联合查询上Precision@5提升8.5个百分点，在否定查询上提升22.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 可靠的胸部X光检索需要训练目标不仅建模哪些发现被提及，还要建模这些发现如何被临床断言（阳性或阴性）。

Abstract: Large chest radiography archives are difficult to search because most studies are paired only with free-text reports rather than structured clinical annotations. Vision-language models offer a natural interface for text-to-image retrieval, but current biomedical models are primarily optimized for report-to-image matching rather than for satisfying short clinical search queries. This creates an objective mismatch: a model may retrieve images related to words in the query while failing to satisfy the full clinical constraint, especially for conjunctions and negations such as ``atelectasis and no pneumonia.''
  We introduce CXR-Retrieve, a structured benchmark for compositional chest X-ray text-to-image retrieval. The benchmark contains 5,159 test images from the official test-split of MIMIC-CXR-JPG and 145 textual queries spanning single and conjunction findings, both positive and negative. Relevance is defined by whether a retrieved image satisfies all asserted pathology constraints, rather than by whether it matches a paired report.
  We further propose a label-aware contrastive fine-tuning objective for clinical retrieval. Our method attracts image-text pairs with compatible asserted pathology constraints, including shared confirmed absences, while explicitly repelling contradictory pairs. Starting from the in-domain CXR-CLIP checkpoint, our method improves Precision@5 over CXR-CLIP by 8.5 percentage points on two-pathology conjunctions and by 22.0 percentage points on negation queries. These results show that reliable chest X-ray retrieval requires training objectives that model not only which findings are mentioned, but also how they are clinically asserted.

</details>


### [31] [FDDWAN: A Frequency-Decoupled Diffusion Network for Watermarking Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27800)
*Chunpeng Wang,Yuxin Li,Xiaoyu Wang,Jidong Yang,Suo Gao,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于频率解耦的扩散水印攻击网络（FDDWAN），通过小波域分解和残差扩散精修的两阶段粗到细框架，实现了水印移除效果与视觉保真度之间更优的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不可见水印移除方法难以准确捕捉含水印的特征，导致水印抑制效果与感知保真度之间存在不利的权衡。

Method: FDDWAN是一个粗到细的两阶段框架。第一阶段，基于小波的频域预攻击模块（WFPAM）将含水印图像分解为低频和高频子带，并针对各自对水印鲁棒性和感知质量的贡献采用频率特定的攻击策略。第二阶段，频域残差扩散攻击模块（FRDAM）在训练时分别建模预攻击输出与无水印参考之间的残差分布，选择性地精修频域残差，将扩散过程引导至剩余的水印相关差异，同时最小化对图像内容的修改。

Result: 在CelebA和ImageNet数据集上，针对四种代表性水印方案的广泛实验表明，FDDWAN相比传统方法和基于学习的方法，能够实现更优的水印移除效果与视觉保真度之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 通过频率解耦设计和残差扩散精修，FDDWAN有效解决了水印抑制与图像内容保留之间的冲突，为不可见水印攻击提供了一种更优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing invisible watermark removal methods often struggle to accurately capture the watermark-bearing features, leading to an unfavorable trade-off between watermark suppression and perceptual fidelity. In this paper, we propose the Frequency-Decoupled Diffusion Watermark Attack Network (FDDWAN), a coarse-to-fine framework that performs watermark removal through wavelet-domain decomposition and residual diffusion refinement. In the initial stage, the Wavelet-based Frequency-domain Preliminary Attack Module (WFPAM) decomposes the watermarked image into low- and high-frequency subbands and applies frequency-specific attack strategies tailored to their respective contributions to watermark robustness and perceptual quality. In the next stage, the Frequency-domain Residual Diffusion Attack Module (FRDAM) separately models the residual distributions between the preliminarily attacked outputs and the corresponding watermark-free references during training. Rather than reconstructing the entire image, FRDAM selectively refines frequency-domain residuals, directing the diffusion process toward the remaining watermark related discrepancies while minimizing modifications to image content. Extensive experiments on CelebA and ImageNet across four representative watermarking schemes demonstrate that FDDWAN achieves a more favorable trade-off between watermark removal effectiveness and visual fidelity than conventional and learning-based attack methods.

</details>


### [32] [LoMeVQA: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Longitudinal Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27806)
*Zhilin Wu,Zhangkai Ni,Chengmei Yang,Longzhen Yang,Yihang Liu,Ying Wen,Lianghua He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LoMeVQA，一个包含206K纵向医学视觉问答对的综合基准，涵盖五个任务（进展分类、进展描述、进展报告生成、差分区域定位、差分区域描述），并引入MedLong-8B模型在全部任务上达到最优性能，揭示了现有多模态大语言模型在时间推理上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上患者多次影像检查产生纵向数据，建模时间信息对疾病进展评估和治疗响应判断至关重要，但多模态大语言模型在纵向医学视觉推理方面仍未得到充分探索，现有方法缺乏此类基准。

Method: 构建自动化流水线：按时间顺序整理患者记录、通过医学知识图谱提取临床有意义实体、建模其时间演化以指导大语言模型生成高质量纵向VQA对。同时提出MedLong-8B模型以解决纵向推理局限。

Result: LoMeVQA包含206K纵向VQA对；通用和医学领域MLLM均表现不佳；MedLong-8B在全部任务上达到最先进性能；详细分析揭示了关键失败模式。

Conclusion: 纵向医学视觉推理是当前MLLM的重要短板，LoMeVQA为此提供了全面基准，MedLong-8B验证了针对性优化的有效性，同时数据分析为改进纵向推理提供了方向。

Abstract: In clinical practice, patients often undergo multiple imaging examinations over successive visits, yielding longitudinal data. Modeling such temporal information is crucial for reliable assessment of disease progression and treatment response. However, despite the rapid advancement of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), longitudinal medical visual reasoning remains largely underexplored. To fill this gap, we propose LoMeVQA, a comprehensive benchmark consisting of 206K longitudinal visual question answering (VQA) pairs for temporal medical image analysis. LoMeVQA covers five tasks: progress classification, progress description, progress report generation, differential region grounding, and differential region description. To construct the dataset, we develop an automated pipeline that (1) organizes patient records chronologically, (2) extracts clinically meaningful entities via a medical knowledge graph, and (3) models their temporal evolution to guide large language models in generating high-quality longitudinal VQA pairs. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that both general-purpose and medical-domain MLLMs perform poorly on LoMeVQA, revealing substantial limitations in temporal reasoning. To address these limitations, we introduce MedLong-8B, which achieves state-of-the-art performance across all tasks. Beyond benchmarking, we conduct detailed analyses that uncover key failure modes and shed light on how to improve longitudinal medical visual reasoning. Our data is available at: https://github.com/pepperbubble/LoMeVQA

</details>


### [33] [SPFM-Net: Semantic-Prior-Guided Frequency-Constrained Mamba for Invisible Watermark Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27811)
*Chunpeng Wang,Yanan Shi,Zhiqiu Xia,Jidong Yang,Suo Gao,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPFM-Net，一种基于语义先验引导和频率约束的Mamba框架用于不可见水印攻击。通过高比例掩蔽、部分微调的预训练MAE、多尺度残差频率特征交互模块和轻量级Mamba全局状态空间特征建模单元，实现对全局分布水印信号的长程依赖捕获，在攻击有效性和视觉保真度之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印攻击方法通常依赖预定义的信号处理操作或局部受限的恢复网络，难以捕获全局分布水印信号的长程依赖关系，导致移除效果与视觉保真度之间存在不佳的权衡。

Method: SPFM-Net包含四个核心组件：(1)高比例掩蔽破坏水印信号的空间相干性；(2)部分微调的预训练MAE从稀疏观测中重建语义一致的图像并抑制水印信息；(3)多尺度残差频率特征交互模块聚合多感受野下的水印相关残差特征，自适应抑制水印无关区域；(4)轻量级Mamba全局状态空间特征建模单元分离水印特征与自然图像内容。此外通过空间域、频率域和边缘域的多级目标联合优化。

Result: 在代表性空间域、变换域、正交矩基和深度学习水印方案上的大量实验表明，SPFM-Net实现了水印攻击有效性与感知保真度之间的良好权衡。

Conclusion: SPFM-Net通过语义先验引导与频率约束的Mamba框架，有效解决了现有水印攻击方法难以捕获全局水印信号长程依赖的问题，在攻击效果和视觉质量之间取得了优越的平衡。

Abstract: Existing watermark attacks typically rely on predefined signal-processing operations or locally constrained restoration networks, making it difficult to capture the long-range dependencies of globally distributed watermark signals and resulting in an unfavorable trade-off between removal effectiveness and visual fidelity. In this paper, we propose SPFM-Net, a semantic-prior-guided and frequency-constrained Mamba framework for invisible watermark attack. SPFM-Net first employs high-ratio masking to disrupt the spatial coherence of invisible watermark signals, and then utilizes a partially fine-tuned pretrained Masked Autoencoder to reconstruct semantically consistent image from sparse observations while suppressing watermark-related information. A Multi-scale Residual Frequency Feature Interaction module subsequently aggregates watermark-related residual features across multiple receptive fields, while adaptively suppressing responses from watermark-irrelevant regions. To further capture the long-range dependencies of globally distributed watermark signals, a lightweight Mamba-based Global State-space Feature Modeling (GSFM) unit is introduced to separate watermark-related features from natural image content and suppress the remaining watermark traces. In addition, SPFM-Net is optimized using a multi-level objective that jointly imposes spatial-, frequency-, and edge-domain constraints, enabling effective watermark suppression while preserving perceptual quality. Extensive experiments on representative spatial-domain, transform-domain, orthogonal moment-based, and deep learning-based watermarking schemes demonstrate that SPFM-Net achieves a favorable trade-off between watermark attack effectiveness and perceptual fidelity.

</details>


### [34] [Hallucinations Leave a Grounding Signature:Verifier-Guided Decoding for Selective Object Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27823)
*Lei Yang,Xinze Liu,Dayan Wu,Ding Wang,Hengjie Zhu,Zihao Zhang,Tianzhu Hu,Hanqi Wu,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Verifier-Guided Decoding (VGD)框架，基于Intrinsic Grounding Signature (IGS)这一分布式带符号注意力模式来检测大型视觉-语言模型(LVLM)生成过程中的对象幻觉。VGD通过轻量级验证器逐对象检查生成内容，识别高风险对象提及后回滚KV缓存、抑制该对象及其同义词并重新生成后续文本。该方法仅对高风险对象进行选择性干预，在显著减少对象幻觉的同时保留了模型的视觉理解能力和真实对象覆盖率，在CHAIR和AMBER-G基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型常常幻觉出图像中不存在的对象。现有缓解方法缺乏可靠的细粒度对象级接地诊断，往往采用粗粒度干预，这会损害视觉理解、缩短回复、降低真实接地对象的覆盖率。关键挑战在于如何在生成过程中检测每个新出现的对象提及是否有可靠的视觉证据支持，以便有选择性地缓解幻觉。此外，输出置信度反映的是下一token的合理性而非视觉支持度，语言先验会使不存在的对象看起来十分确定。

Method: 作者首先发现缺失的诊断证据编码在Intrinsic Grounding Signature (IGS)中——一种分布式的带符号注意力模式，即使对高置信度的幻觉也保持信息量。基于IGS，提出Verifier-Guided Decoding (VGD)框架：一个轻量级验证器检查每个新出现的对象提及，当该提及被识别为高风险时回滚KV缓存，抑制该对象及其同义词，并重新生成受影响的后续文本。VGD仅对高风险对象提及进行干预，从而在减少对象幻觉的同时保留模型原有的视觉理解能力和接地对象覆盖率。

Result: 在CHAIR和AMBER-G基准上，VGD实现了最先进的对象幻觉减少效果：在@rec90时，AMBER-G CHAIR减少了43.6%，同时保留了99.6%的接地对象覆盖率；CHAIR-MSCOCO的CHAIR_i/CHAIR_s分别减少了37.0%/30.4%，且不缩短描述长度。

Conclusion: VGD通过细粒度的对象级选择性干预，在显著降低LVLM对象幻觉的同时几乎不影响真实接地对象的覆盖率和输出长度，验证了IGS作为可靠接地诊断信号的有效性，为LVLM解码阶段的幻觉缓解提供了更精准的新范式。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate objects that are absent from an image. Despite recent progress, existing mitigation methods still lack reliable object-level grounding diagnostics and therefore tend to apply coarse-grained interventions, which can impair visual understanding, shorten responses, and reduce coverage of genuinely grounded objects. The key challenge is thus to detect, during generation, whether each emerging object mention is supported by reliable visual evidence, so that hallucination can be mitigated selectively. Yet output confidence reflects next-token plausibility rather than visual support, allowing language priors to make absent objects appear certain. We show that the missing diagnostic evidence is encoded in an Intrinsic Grounding Signature (IGS), a distributed signed attention pattern that remains informative for such confident hallucinations. Based on IGS, we propose Verifier-Guided Decoding (VGD), a decoding framework in which a lightweight verifier examines each emerging object mention, rolls back the KV cache when the mention is identified as high risk, suppresses the object and its synonyms, and regenerates the affected continuation. Because VGD intervenes only on object mentions identified as high risk, it reduces object hallucination while preserving the model's original visual understanding and grounded object coverage. Experiments on CHAIR and AMBER-G show that VGD achieves state-of-the-art object hallucination reduction: at @rec90, it cuts AMBER-G CHAIR by 43.6\% while retaining 99.6\% of grounded-object coverage, and reduces CHAIR-MSCOCO CHAIR$_i$/CHAIR$_s$ by 37.0\%/30.4\% without shortening captions.

</details>


### [35] [Thinking Once Is Enough: Intermediate-Layer Evidence Routing for High-Resolution VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27830)
*Zhongkuan Mao,Xianjie Liu,Tianyu Meng,Yidong Wang,Wenzhuo Zhao,Ronghao Xian,Yao Jiang,Fei Shen,Junfeng Fang,Yong Dai,Yi Zhang,Keren Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Thinking-Once，一种免训练、单次视觉输入的证据路由方法，通过重建中间层问题条件注意力来保留细粒度证据并路由到后续层，无需额外视觉编码即可提升HR-VQA性能，在多个基准上平均提升约3分，同时降低峰值内存约4GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HR-VQA方法将问题视为证据获取不足，依赖裁剪、重编码或多轮搜索等方式反复获取视觉输入。作者认为这种观点不完整：细粒度证据在视觉编码后仍保留在中间层路由窗口中，但随后被稀释。因此需要一种无需额外视觉输入即可恢复和利用这些既有证据的方法。

Method: Thinking-Once在中间层重建问题条件注意力，保留核心实体标记和紧凑背景上下文，并将这些证据路由到后续层，全过程免训练且只进行一次视觉编码（single-visual-pass）。

Result: 在5个基础模型上一致提升或匹配基线，V*Bench、HRBench-4K和HRBench-8K平均分别提升+3.1、+3.0和+2.7分，峰值内存降低约4GB。在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上提升+9.9、+4.6和+5.5分，跨基准均值从72.5提升至79.1；在ZwZ-8B上达到82.7均值。对比11个开源基线，在所有三个基准上取得最佳或并列最佳成绩，相比DeepScan推理时间降低97.2%。

Conclusion: HR-VQA可以通过路由已有编码证据而非反复获取新的视觉输入来改进，这挑战了传统认知中"HR-VQA必须通过更多视觉采样来解决"的范式。

Abstract: High-resolution visual question answering (HR-VQA) is often treated as a problem of insufficient evidence acquisition, where failing multimodal large language models must inspect images again through cropping, re-encoding, or multi-round search. We show that this view is incomplete: in many cases, fine-grained evidence has already survived visual encoding and become identifiable and influential within an intermediate-layer routing window, but is later diluted before answer generation. We propose Thinking-Once, a \textbf{training-free, single-visual-pass} evidence-routing method that reconstructs question-conditioned attention at this window, preserves core entity tokens and compact background context, and routes this evidence to later layers without extra visual encoding. Across five base models, Thinking-Once consistently improves or matches the corresponding base setting, increasing the average scores on V$^*$Bench, HRBench-4K, and HRBench-8K by \textit{+3.1}, \textit{+3.0}, and \textit{+2.7} points while reducing the average peak memory by about 4,GB. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, it improves the three benchmarks by \textit{+9.9}, \textit{+4.6}, and \textit{+5.5} points, raising the cross-benchmark mean from 72.5 to 79.1. With the ZwZ-8B base model, Thinking-Once reaches a mean score of 82.7. Against 11 open-source HR-VQA baselines, it obtains the best or tied-best score on all three benchmark averages and the best overall mean; for example, compared with DeepScan, it reduces V$^*$Bench inference time by \textbf{97.2\%} while improving the cross-benchmark mean from 77.8 to 79.1. These results show that HR-VQA can be improved by routing already encoded evidence rather than repeatedly acquiring new visual inputs. Code is available in the appendix.

</details>


### [36] [SAFViT: Spatial Attention Fusion Gating for Vision Transformer-Based Nucleus Segmentation and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27835)
*Harshit Mittal,Arash Rabbani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Spatial Attention Fusion (SAF) Gating模块，用于替代CellViT模型中的传统跳跃连接。该模块通过拼接编码器跳跃特征和上采样的解码器特征，经两次逐点卷积和ReLU压缩后，应用通道级softmax生成逐像素的"信任热力图"，使网络能够学习每个空间位置最可信的特征来源，从而提升细胞分割与分类的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的直接跳跃连接平等对待每个空间位置，导致冗余甚至冲突的信息传入解码器。现有的门控机制大多仅过滤编码器信息，忽略了来自解码器的全局上下文信息，限制了模型的性能提升。

Method: 在CellViT模型中用SAF Gating模块替代传统跳跃连接。每个SAF门将编码器跳跃特征与上采样的解码器特征拼接，通过两次带中间ReLU的逐点卷积进行压缩，再应用通道级softmax生成逐像素的信任热力图（每个空间位置各来源的权重和为1），实现自适应特征融合。

Result: 在PanNuke和MoNuSeg数据集上与六种门控方案（无门控、注意力门、SENet、CBAM、交叉注意力、注意力特征融合）对比，SAF Gating取得了最高的mPQ值0.471。该增益主要由Dead类F1分数相比无门控CellViT基线提升14.5个百分点驱动。

Conclusion: SAF Gating通过引入解码器全局上下文信息并生成逐像素信任权重，有效改善了模型对少数类"Dead"的检测能力，从而显著提升了多类全景分割质量（mPQ），验证了融合解码器上下文信息的门控机制的优越性。

Abstract: Accurate cell segmentation and classification are foundational to digital pathology, enabling quantitative tissue analysis for diagnosis and treatment planning. Encoder-decoder architectures that fuse multi-scale features through skip connections have become the dominant paradigm for this task, yet standard direct skip connections treat every spatial location equally, which leads to redundant and potentially conflicting information reaching the decoder. To overcome this problem, various gating mechanisms have been introduced, but most of them operate solely on filtering encoder information, neglecting the benefit of global contextual information from the decoder. This study proposes replacing conventional skip connections in a CellViT-based model with a novel Spatial Attention Fusion (SAF) Gating module. Each SAF gate concatenates the encoder skip and upsampled decoder features, compresses them through two pointwise convolutions with an intermediate ReLU, and applies a channel-wise softmax to produce a per-pixel "heatmap of trust" that sums to unity at every spatial location, allowing the network to learn where each source is most trustworthy. The resulting fused features improve the model's ability to detect the minority "Dead" class, which in turn enhances the multi-class panoptic quality (mPQ) on the PanNuke dataset. SAF Gating is compared against six gating alternatives including no gating, attention gates, squeeze-and-excitation, CBAM, cross-attention, and attentional feature fusion on PanNuke and MoNuSeg datasets. SAF Gating achieves the highest mPQ (0.471), a gain driven primarily by a 14.5-point improvement in Dead-class F1 score compared to ungated CellViT baseline.

</details>


### [37] [FeatFix: Reuse What You Verify through Local Exact-Feature Correction for Faster Cached Diffusion Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27842)
*Hanshuai Cui,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Qianli Ma,Fanshuai Meng,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FeatFix，一种面向扩散模型缓存推理的局部精确特征校正方法。通过复用验证时计算出的精确特征来替换草稿块输出，在固定稀疏的层-时间步站点进行校正，无需部分替换或全时间步重计算，在四个图像和视频骨干网络上实现最高6.70倍加速且保持输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型迭代去噪过程计算量大，现有训练无关的加速方法通过缓存或预测中间特征来降低成本。但已有方法中，用于验证草稿漂移而计算的精确特征仅用于度量差异或指导后续决策后被丢弃，未充分挖掘其复用价值。

Method: FeatFix在固定的稀疏层-时间步站点上，将完整的草稿块输出替换为从相同输入状态计算出的精确输出。该方法避免了令牌或通道级别的部分替换以及全时间步重计算，通过在被选中的验证站点复用精确特征来重置局部草稿残差，从而减少下游特征误差。

Result: 在四个图像和视频骨干网络上进行实验，FeatFix相对于Vanilla推理实现最高6.70倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的输出质量。

Conclusion: 先前验证时计算的精确特征可被复用用于校正而非仅仅丢弃，基于此观察的FeatFix以局部精确特征校正方式有效加速缓存扩散推理，在速度与质量之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion models are widely used to generate high-quality images and videos, but their iterative denoising process remains computationally intensive. A growing class of training-free accelerators reduces this cost by reusing cached intermediate features or forecasting future ones. To control draft drift, these methods sometimes compute an exact block feature for verification. Yet the resulting exact feature is typically used only to measure discrepancy or guide a later decision and is then discarded. We find that this previously computed feature can instead be reused for correction. Forwarding it at the verification site resets the local draft residual and reduces downstream feature error. Based on this observation, we introduce FeatFix, a local exact-feature correction method for cached diffusion inference. FeatFix operates at a fixed sparse set of layer--timestep sites. At each selected site, it replaces the complete draft block output with the exact output computed from the same incoming state, avoiding token- or channel-level partial replacement and full-timestep recomputation. Experiments across four image and video backbones show that FeatFix consistently accelerates generation, achieving a speedup of up to $6.70\times$ over Vanilla while maintaining competitive output quality.

</details>


### [38] [VCP-DCN: Beyond Visual Concealed Property via Depth Collaborative Network for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27843)
*Songsong Duan,Xi Yang,Nannan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VCP-DCN深度协作网络，通过渐进式的多模态对齐、交互与融合，挖掘深度域中可区分的多模态特征，以提升伪装目标检测（COD）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有COD方法引入深度图以提升检测性能，但忽略了深度域中隐蔽目标特有的模态特征，未能充分利用多模态信息的互补性。

Method: VCP-DCN包含三个阶段：1）对齐阶段，提出分离原型嵌入（SPE）模块，通过原型对比学习获得模态一致性和模态特异性的RGB/深度原型token；2）交互阶段，提出多模态双注意力（MDA）模块，利用模态一致性原型token与视觉token之间的局部响应图增强跨模态特征表示；3）融合阶段，提出深度自适应注入（DAI）模块，通过决策机制计算RGB/深度模态特异性原型token与模态一致性token之间的相似度距离，自适应评估特征的贡献。

Result: 在三个权威数据集上的大量实验验证了VCP-DCN的有效性。

Conclusion: VCP-DCN通过深度域中的视觉隐蔽原型挖掘可区分的多模态特征，有效解决了现有RGB-D COD方法忽视深度模态特性的问题。

Abstract: Camouflaged Object Detection (COD) aims to identify and segment camouflaged objects in complex environments, which are often concealed because their color and texture are similar to the background. Several existing COD methods introduce depth maps to boost detection performance via learning complementary RGB-D features, ignoring modality-specific characteristics of concealed objects in the depth domain. To address this issue, we propose a depth collaborative network, called VCP-DCN, to mine distinguishable multi-modality features beyond visual concealed prototype in depth domain. Specifically, VCP-DCN progressively performs multi-modality alignment, interaction, and fusion for the COD task. In the \textbf{alignment} stage, we propose a Separable Prototype Embedding (SPE) module to learn modality-consistency and modality-specific RGB/depth prototype tokens through prototype contrastive learning. Furthermore, we develop a Multi-modality Dual Attention (MDA) module to enhance the cross-modal feature representation through local response maps between modality-consistency RGB/depth prototype tokens and visual tokens on the \textbf{interaction} stage. Finally, we design a Depth Adaptive Injection (DAI) module to adaptively measure contribution of RGB/depth features with a decision-making mechanism, which calculates similarity distance between RGB/depth modality-specific prototype tokens and modality-consistency ones on the \textbf{fusion} stage. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our VCP-DCN on three authoritative datasets.

</details>


### [39] [Benchmarking Foundation and Large Language Models for Few-Shot Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27856)
*Jinghong Liu,Yuchuan Deng,Fanping Liu,Meng Huang,Xirong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FAME，一个统一的少样本医学图像分割（FS-MIS）基准，涵盖7个解剖部位、9种成像模态、14个ROI类别，共14,958个测试样本，在零样本和十样本设置下对四类方法（专用分割模型、SAM类、CLIP类、MLLM类）进行系统评估，并揭示了支持样本利用策略、样本数量增益、语义迁移难度等多个关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FS-MIS方法虽然发展迅速，但跨越多个不同范式（专业人员、SAM、CLIP、MLLM等），且在不同且不一致的评估条件下被测试，导致它们之间的相对有效性不清晰，缺乏一个统一、全面的基准来进行公平比较和系统分析。

Method: 构建名为FAME的统一基准测试平台：(1) 覆盖7个解剖部位、9种成像模态、14个ROI类别的14,958个测试样本；(2) 评估四类FS-MIS方法（专门分割器、基于SAM的方法、基于CLIP的方法、基于MLLM的方法）；(3) 在零样本和十样本两种设置下评估；(4) 额外评估目标缺失识别能力以及协变量偏移和语义偏移下的泛化性能。

Result: 评估发现三个关键结论：其一，有效的小样本分割取决于模型如何利用支持样本，直接视觉适应通常优于基于提示的策略；其二，增加支持样本数量仅在模型能有效利用时才会提升性能；其三，语义迁移比成像域适应更具挑战性，且强定位能力不一定意味着可靠的目标缺失识别能力。

Conclusion: FAME提供了对当前FS-MIS解决方案的全面理解，有助于推动更有效、更可靠的少样本医学分割方法的发展。

Abstract: Few-shot medical image segmentation (FS-MIS) aims to segment novel regions of interest (ROIs) from a few annotated support examples. Despite rapid progress, existing FS-MIS solutions span diverse paradigms but are evaluated under inconsistent settings, leaving their relative effectiveness unclear. We introduce FAME, a unified benchmark for evaluating FS-MIS solutions, covering specialists, SAM-based methods, CLIP-based methods, and MLLM-based methods. FAME contains 14,958 test samples across 7 anatomical sites, 9 imaging modalities, and 14 ROI categories, and evaluates models under zero-shot and ten-shot settings with additional assessment of target-absence recognition and generalization under covariate and semantic shifts. Our evaluation reveals several findings. First, effective few-shot segmentation depends on how models exploit support examples: direct visual adaptation generally outperforms prompt-based strategies. Second, increasing support examples improves performance only when models can effectively utilize them. Third, semantic transfer remains substantially more challenging than imaging-domain adaptation, and strong localization ability does not necessarily imply reliable target-absence recognition. We hope FAME provides a comprehensive understanding of current FS-MIS solutions and facilitates the development of more effective and reliable few-shot medical segmentation methods.

</details>


### [40] [EEG-EditBench: Probing Visual Information in EEG-Image Retrieval Models with Controlled Image Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27857)
*Kaifan Zhang,Lihuo He,Yuqi Ji,Junjie Ke,Lukun Wu,Tianhao You,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EEG-EditBench，一个用于诊断EEG视觉解码模型究竟"看到了什么"的基准测试。它通过对测试图像进行受控编辑（对象身份、属性、背景、对象存在性），评估模型能否区分原始图像与其编辑版本。结果显示，在标准检索上表现优异的模型并不能一致地转移到基于编辑的评估上，细粒度属性变化最具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG-to-image检索模型虽然在语义多样的候选集中表现良好，但无法揭示模型到底依赖哪些视觉信息进行匹配。一个模型可能轻松识别出猎豹（vs工具/植物/车辆），却无法区分猎豹原图与将猎豹换成狗的编辑图。这种"检索准确率掩盖了模型对视觉细节的感知能力"的问题，促使作者提出受控编辑的诊断基准。

Method: 从200 THINGS-EEG2测试图像出发，通过四类受控编辑（对象身份、对象属性、背景、对象存在性）构建包含2,137个质量受控编辑的图像集。在分类和属性编辑的基础上，对八个代表性EEG视觉解码模型进行系统评估，比较标准检索与编辑辨别任务的性能差异。

Result: 强标准检索性能不能一致迁移到基于编辑的评估上；细粒度属性变化对模型最具挑战性。EEG-EditBench揭示出被聚合检索准确率所隐藏的模型行为差异。

Conclusion: EEG-EditBench为研究EEG图像模型保留了哪些视觉信息提供了受控基准，代码和完整数据集已公开，可作为未来EEG视觉解码研究标准化诊断的工具。

Abstract: Recent EEG-to-image retrieval models have achieved strong performance in identifying viewed images from semantically diverse candidates. Yet such success does not reveal what visual information supports the match. A model may readily identify a cheetah among tools, plants, and vehicles, but can it still distinguish the viewed cheetah from the same scene with the cheetah replaced by a dog? Motivated by this question, we introduce EEG-EditBench, a diagnostic benchmark that examines this question through controlled edits of object identity, attributes, background, and object presence. Built from the 200 THINGS-EEG2 test images, EEG-EditBench contains 2,137 quality-controlled edits and evaluates eight representative EEG visual decoding models. Our results show that strong standard retrieval does not consistently transfer to edit-based evaluation, with fine-grained attribute changes presenting the greatest challenge. EEG-EditBench reveals model behavior hidden by aggregate retrieval accuracy and provides a controlled basis for studying what visual information EEG-image models preserve. The code and complete dataset are publicly available.

</details>

*Dragos Costea,Alina Marcu,Cristina Lazar,Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Drones2BodyLanguage数据集，将具有十种交流意图的虚拟人物以度量精确的位姿嵌入到真实4K无人机航拍场景中，解决无人机社会智能感知中人体运动与意图数据匮乏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程感知人类运动与意图是社交智能空中机器人的前提，但用于学习该能力的数据几乎不存在。无人机航拍场景下缺乏标注了人体运动与意图意图的数据集，制约了相关模型训练。

Method: 构建一个轻量级局部场景几何世界模型：通过语义选择的锚点结合流式单目深度估计将其提升到3D，将放置点预测为仿射锚点组合（具有可证明的刚体不变权重），并通过SVD拟合的地面旋转进行重新渲染，实现虚拟人物在未经修改的4K航拍场景中具有度量正确的位置、尺度和朝向，并维持数百帧相机运动的一致性。

Result: 在12种架构、场景与运动分离的数据划分上，基于放置数据训练可将真实、重定向和生成运动的平均意图识别准确率大幅提升，并在两个野外场景中确认了增益。

Conclusion: Drones2BodyLanguage通过合成放置的具身运动数据，有效弥合了无人机视角下人体意图识别训练数据缺失的空白，并在多种架构与运动来源上显著提升了意图识别性能。

Abstract: Perceiving human motion and intent at long range is a prerequisite for socially intelligent aerial robots, yet the data to learn it barely exists. We introduce Drones2BodyLanguage, a dataset grounding human motion in real UAV footage: avatars manifesting ten communicative intents are placed into unmodified 4K drone scenes with metrically correct position, scale and orientation, maintained over hundreds of frames of camera motion. Enabling it is a lightweight geometric world model of the local scene - semantically selected anchors lifted to 3D through streaming monocular depth - in which a placement point is predicted as an affine anchor combination with provably rigid-invariant weights, and re-rendered under an SVD-fitted ground rotation. Across twelve architectures on scene- and motion-disjoint splits, training on placed data lifts mean intent accuracy by a wide margin for real, retargeted and generated motion alike, with gains confirmed on two in-the-wild scenes.

</details>


### [42] [DECODE: Tackling Representation and Decision Degradation in Continual AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27882)
*Zihao Cai,Xinghan Li,Ruiyan Yang,Xue Song,Haijun Shan,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DECODE框架，解决AI生成图像检测器在持续学习场景中的双重遗忘问题（表示退化与决策边界漂移），通过子空间多样性正则化（SDR）和闭式决策对齐（CDA）在19个生成域上达到99.36%的平均准确率且仅0.39%的遗忘率，并能泛化到11个未见生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法主要将灾难性遗忘视为表示层面的问题，通过稳定特征表示来解决。但本文指出该视角不完整：即使特征表示保持判别性，持续优化分类头也会导致决策边界逐渐漂移。这两种效应共同形成"双重退化"（Dual Degradation）的复合失败模式，现有方法未能同时解决。

Method: 提出解耦的持续检测框架DECODE，包含两个核心组件：（1）子空间多样性正则化（SDR），用于保持多样的取证特征表示；（2）闭式决策对齐（CDA），在每次适配器合并后以无需手动超参数调优的方式重新校准共享分类头。框架将表示层面与决策层面的遗忘缓解进行解耦处理。

Result: 在19个生成域上的实验表明，DECODE达到99.36%的平均准确率，遗忘率仅为0.39%；同时能泛化至11个未见生成器，准确率达95.36%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 研究验证了"双重退化"现象的存在，证明仅关注表示层面的遗忘缓解是不够的，必须同时处理决策边界漂移。DECODE通过解耦表示与决策层面的遗忘缓解，实现了高性能的持续AI生成图像检测。

Abstract: As generative models continue to evolve, AI-generated image detectors must incrementally adapt to emerging generative domains while preserving knowledge acquired from previous ones. This continual learning setting is particularly challenging because forensic traces are often subtle and generator-specific, making detectors highly vulnerable to catastrophic forgetting. Existing methods primarily address this problem by stabilizing feature representations, implicitly treating forgetting as a representation-level issue. In this paper, we show that this perspective is incomplete. We demonstrate that even when feature representations remain discriminative, the decision boundary can progressively drift as the classification head is continually optimized on new domains. These two effects jointly give rise to a compound failure mode, termed Dual Degradation. To overcome this challenge, we propose DECODE, a decoupled continual detection framework that jointly mitigates representation- and decision-level forgetting. Specifically, we introduce Subspace Diversity Regularization (SDR) to preserve diverse forensic representations and Closed-Form Decision Alignment (CDA) to recalibrate the shared classification head after each adapter merge without manual hyperparameter tuning. Extensive experiments on 19 generative domains show that DECODE achieves an average accuracy of 99.36% with only 0.39% forgetting, while further generalizing to 11 unseen generators with 95.36% accuracy.

</details>


### [43] [Unifying Adversarially Robust Model Experts in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27897)
*Nguyen Duc Thai,Junhao Dong,Sua Qi Rong,Hua Yu,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE框架，通过嵌入空间对齐实现多个对抗微调鲁棒模型专家之间的协作学习，最终将知识整合为单一统一鲁棒模型，在图像分类和视觉语言下游任务上优于单独训练的专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（如CLIP）易受对抗攻击威胁，对抗微调是重要防御手段，但不同微调策略产生的模型具有不同的鲁棒性特征，各自在某些评测场景表现优异而在其他场景失效，单一的专家模型无法全面防御。

Method: 提出CARE框架：在训练过程中维护多个专家模型，通过嵌入空间对齐实现知识交换，同时保留各专家的个性化专长，最后将学到的知识整合为单一统一鲁棒模型。该框架在两种具有互补鲁棒性行为的对抗微调策略上进行验证。

Result: 在经典图像分类和下游视觉语言任务上，CARE均优于单独训练的模型专家，证明协作学习能继承互补的鲁棒性性质。

Conclusion: 跨模型专家的协作学习是提升对抗鲁棒性的有前景方向。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), such as CLIP, are vulnerable to adversarial attacks, posing a serious problem for real-life applications and deployment. Adversarial fine-tuning emerges as a prominent defense method; however, different fine-tuning strategies often produce specialized models with distinct robustness characteristics. Each fine-tuned model in turn thrives in some evaluation settings but falters on others, limiting their defensive capabilities. We refer to these specialized fine-tuned models as robust model experts and propose a collaborative adversarial fine-tuning framework: CARE - Collaborative Adversarial Robustness fine-tuning using Embedding alignment. CARE maintains multiple experts during training, enables knowledge exchange through embedding-space harmonization, and consolidates the learned knowledge into a single unified robust model. Experts benefit from one another while preserving their individual specializations, enabling the final model to inherit complementary robustness properties. In this paper, we demonstrate CARE on two different adversarial fine-tuning strategies with complementary robustness behaviors. Extensive experiments on classic image classification and downstream vision-language tasks display the effectiveness of our approach, with CARE being able to outperform individually learned model experts. The results suggest that collaborative learning across model experts is a promising direction for improving adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [44] [CoRE-UIR: Prior-guided common and residual experts for efficient all-in-one remote sensing image restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27898)
*Zaiyan Zhang,Qiangqiang Yuan,Jie Li,Ziyang Lihe,Yu Wan,Yuzeng Chen,Xin Su,Liangpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoRE-UIR，一种基于通用与残差专家（CoRE）的先验引导全局-局部一体化图像复原框架，显式分解复原能力为通用密集专家和低秩残差专家，结合CLIP先验嵌入与全局特征调制，并构建大规模无人机多退化数据集MDVD-108K，在性能、速度和显存占用上全面超越最强基线BaryIR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机和卫星遥感图像常面临多重退化（天气、光照、成像伪影）同时发生的问题，现有All-in-One复原方法存在退化线索弱或隐式、以及全秩多专家设计导致参数冗余和复原行为重叠等缺陷，需要更高效且显式的统一复原方案。

Method: 提出CoRE（Common-and-Residual Expert Block），将复原能力分解为通用密集专家（处理退化不变复原）和低秩残差专家（处理退化特定补偿），避免冗余专家复制；配合Degradation Prior Embedding（DPE）利用冻结CLIP特征引入显式复原先验，以及Global Feature Modulation（GFM）在局部残差补偿前对齐全局特征统计量。同时构建MDVD-108K多退化数据集。

Result: 在多个数据集上，CoRE-UIR平均PSNR提升1.05 dB，推理速度比最强基线BaryIR快11.83倍，峰值显存减少85.3%，并在下游任务和未见退化上验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: CoRE-UIR通过在通用与残差专家间进行显式能力分解，实现了高质量与高效率的良好平衡，为通用图像复原提供了一种更具可扩展性和资源高效的范式。

Abstract: Remote sensing images acquired by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and satellites are often degraded by adverse weather, illumination variation, and imaging artifacts, which may co-occur and jointly induce global distribution shifts and local structural corruption. Although All-in-One image restoration offers an appealing unified alternative to task-specific pipelines, existing methods still suffer from weak or implicit degradation cues and parameter redundancy caused by full-rank multi-expert designs with overlapping restoration behaviors. We propose CoRE-UIR (Common and Residual Experts for Universal Image Restoration), a prior-guided global-local framework centered on the Common-and-Residual Expert Block (CoRE). CoRE explicitly decomposes restoration capacity into a common dense expert for degradation-invariant restoration and low-rank residual experts for degradation-specific compensation, enabling adaptive specialization without redundant expert replication. Built on this design, Degradation Prior Embedding (DPE) adapts frozen CLIP features into an explicit restoration-oriented prior, while Global Feature Modulation (GFM) aligns global feature statistics before local residual compensation. We also construct MDVD-108K (Multi-Degradation VisDrone), a large-scale UAV restoration dataset covering both single and compound degradations, together with a real-world test set. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets show that CoRE-UIR improves the overall average PSNR by 1.05 dB while running 11.83$\times$ faster and reducing peak memory by 85.3% relative to the strongest baseline, BaryIR, thereby maintaining a favorable quality-efficiency trade-off. Evaluations on downstream tasks and unseen degradation also validate the generalizability of CoRE-UIR. The code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/zzaiyan/CoRE-UIR.

</details>


### [45] [One Patch Is Enough: Reinforcement-Optimized Visual Token Grounding for MLLM-Based Scene Text Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27902)
*Rui Tang,Wentao Yang,Peirong Zhang,Yongxin Shi,Shun Zhang,Huiguo He,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPaTS是一种基于MLLM的场景文字识别框架，通过单一锚点视觉token路由每个文本实例，结合强化学习优化离散token选择（SPaSO）、方向嵌入对齐（DEA）和patch增强解码（PED），在场景文字识别任务上显著超越前沿闭源MLLM和OCR MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景文字识别需要文本识别与空间定位之间的高精度对齐。虽然视觉token grounding已成为MLLM的有前景范式，但现有multi-patch范式在密集或小文本实例场景中会引入冗余噪声和定位模糊问题。

Method: 提出SPaTS框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Single-Patch Selective Optimization (SPaSO)：基于强化学习框架，利用patch级奖励优化离散视觉token选择，无需oracle标签即可准确定位anchor；(2) Directional Embedding Alignment (DEA)：通过解耦特征幅值和方向来抑制不稳定的范数偏差，提升表示鲁棒性；(3) Patch-Enhanced Decoding (PED)：将路由的anchor与语言语义融合，并在整幅图像特征图上进行跨注意力，实现超越坐标空间替代方案的几何感知边界回归。

Result: 大量实验表明SPaTS在场景文字识别任务上始终且显著优于前沿闭源MLLM和OCR MLLM。

Conclusion: SPaTS通过单一锚点token范式有效解决了multi-patch grounding的冗余噪声和定位模糊问题，验证了视觉中心化框架在MLLM场景文字识别中的高效性与优越性，代码将开源。

Abstract: Scene text spotting requires high-precision alignment between textual recognition and spatial localization. While visual-token grounding has emerged as a promising formulation for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), the previous multi-patch paradigm often introduces redundant noise and localization ambiguity, particularly for dense or small text instances. To address this, we propose Single-Patch Text Spotting (SPaTS), a vision-centric framework that routes each text instance through a single anchor visual token and then recovers geometry via full-image refinement. To accurately identify this anchor without oracle labels, we introduce Single-Patch Selective Optimization (SPaSO), a reinforcement learning framework that optimizes discrete visual-token selection using patch-level rewards. To further improve representation robustness and localization precision, we introduce Directional Embedding Alignment (DEA) to suppress unstable norm bias by decoupling feature magnitude and direction, and Patch-Enhanced Decoding (PED) to fuse the routed anchor with language semantics and cross-attend over the full-image feature map for geometry-aware boundary regression beyond coordinate-space surrogates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPaTS consistently and significantly outperforms both frontier closed-source MLLMs and OCR MLLMs. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [46] [ARD-REFSM: Enhancing Reflection Symmetry Detection with Asymmetric Denoising and Rotation Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27927)
*Dongfu Yin,Rourou Su,Cong Zhao,Fei Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种反射对称性检测方法，包含非对称区域去噪（ARD）模块和旋转等变特征相似性匹配（REFSM）模块，并引入新基准数据集GMSYM，在四个标准数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射对称性检测面临两大挑战：非对称区域的背景杂波干扰对称模式匹配，以及传统卷积神经网络缺乏旋转等变性导致旋转变换下的特征表示不一致。

Method: 提出两个核心模块：1）非对称区域去噪（ARD）模块，抑制非对称干扰以精炼对称模式；2）旋转等变特征相似性匹配（REFSM）模块，采用双输入框架，通过旋转损失最大化原始图像与旋转图像得分图之间的一致性，从而实现旋转等变对称轴的精确预测。此外构建了包含多样化场景和各种干扰的新基准数据集GMSYM。

Result: 在四个标准数据集（DENDI、NYU、LDRS、SDRW）以及提出的GMSYM数据集上进行的大量实验表明，该方法在准确性和鲁棒性方面均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法通过联合处理非对称干扰和旋转等变性问题，有效提升了反射对称性检测的精度与鲁棒性，GMSYM基准数据集进一步弥补了现有基准的不足。

Abstract: Reflection symmetry detection remains challenging due to interference from asymmetric regions and arbitrary orientations of symmetric patterns. Asymmetric regions introduce background clutter that disrupts symmetric pattern matching, whereas conventional convolutional neural networks lack rotation equivariance, leading to inconsistent feature representations under rotational transformations. To address these issues, we propose an Asymmetric Region Denoising (ARD) module and a Rotation Equivariant Feature Similarity Matching (REFSM) module. The ARD module suppresses asymmetric interference to refine symmetric patterns, while the REFSM module enhances rotation equivariance through feature similarity matching between original and rotated images. Specifically, our dual-input REFSM framework leverages rotation loss to maximize consistency between the score maps of original and rotated images, thereby enabling precise prediction of rotation-equivariant symmetry axes. Furthermore, we introduce GMSYM, a new benchmark dataset that categorizes images into diverse scenarios and incorporates various interferences to address the limitations of existing reflection symmetry detection benchmarks. Extensive experiments on four standard datasets (DENDI, NYU, LDRS, SDRW) and our proposed GMSYM dataset demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [47] [LAST: The Last Query Token Guides Visual Token Pruning for Edge-Cloud Collaborative MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27952)
*Feng Yang,Xinrui Ju,Keyang Zhang,Xiandong Meng,Rongqun Lin,Howard Leung,Shiqi Wang,Haoliang Li,Chris Xing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LAST，一种训练自由的查询相关视觉token剪枝框架，用于边缘-云协作的MLLM推理。利用边缘侧VLM作为引导代理，仅从最后一个查询token对视觉token的注意力中提取轻量级重要性信号，无需访问云模型内部状态、无需自回归生成或昂贵的注意力聚合，在20个token预算下保留95.4%的全token精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘-云协作的多模态基础模型将视觉输入转化为token序列供云端MLLM处理，但密集的视觉token序列显著增加云端推理成本。现有剪枝方法要么是查询无关的（视觉驱动），要么依赖目标MLLM的内部状态（无法在传输前确定token重要性），无法有效应用于边缘-云场景。

Method: LAST使用紧凑的边缘侧VLM作为引导代理（guidance proxy），利用因果注意力机制下最后查询token能关注全部视觉序列和完整查询上下文这一特性，仅从最后查询token对视觉token的注意力权重中推导轻量级重要性信号，实现查询感知的token剪枝。在固定token预算下，保留一组多样化的查询相关视觉token，全程无需云模型访问、无需自回归生成、无需对多个查询位置做昂贵聚合。

Result: 在11个多模态基准、多种token预算下与不同引导策略的剪枝方法对比，LAST一致取得最优性能：仅保留12.5%的视觉token即可保持95.4%的全token精度，边缘侧选择开销低且云侧计算量显著降低。

Conclusion: LAST证明仅利用最后查询token的注意力信号即可在边缘侧实现高效的查询感知视觉token剪枝，无需访问云模型内部状态，为边缘-云协作MLLM推理提供了训练自由、低开销且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models are reshaping edge-cloud visual intelligence from task-specific feature pipelines into token-based interfaces, where edge devices encode visual inputs into tokens for a general-purpose cloud MLLM. However, dense visual-token sequences increase cloud-side inference costs. Existing pruning methods mainly target centralized inference: vision-driven methods can operate before cloud execution but are typically query-agnostic, whereas query-guided methods often rely on internal states of the target MLLM and cannot determine token relevance before transmission. Compact guidance models offer an alternative, but existing designs may require costly attention aggregation or auxiliary generation. We propose LAST, a training-free framework for query-dependent visual token pruning in edge-cloud collaborative MLLM inference. LAST uses a compact edge-side VLM as a guidance proxy and derives a lightweight importance signal from the last query token's attention to visual tokens. Under causal attention, the last query token can attend to the full visual sequence and the entire query context, enabling query-aware pruning without cloud-model access, autoregressive generation, or costly aggregation over multiple query positions. LAST then retains a diverse set of query-relevant visual tokens under a fixed token budget. We evaluate LAST on 11 multimodal benchmarks under multiple token budgets against pruning methods with different guidance strategies. Experiments show that LAST consistently achieves the strongest performance, preserving 95.4% of the full-token accuracy while retaining only 12.5% of the visual tokens, with low edge-side selection overhead and reduced cloud-side computation.

</details>


### [48] [FiRE: Enhancing MLLMs with Fine-Grained Context Learning for Complex Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27959)
*Bohan Hou,Haoqiang Lin,Xuemeng Song,Haokun Wen,Meng Liu,Yupeng Hu,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化的细粒度多模态五元组数据构建流水线和一种新颖的两阶段细粒度多模态微调策略，以增强多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在图像检索任务中的性能，特别是在长文本到图像检索、视觉对话检索和组合图像检索（CIR）等复杂任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型虽然具有强大的通用多模态处理和推理能力，可作为通用图像检索器，但先前的研究忽视了细粒度上下文建模和解耦的微调目标在提升检索性能方面的潜力，尤其在长文本到图像检索、视觉对话检索和组合图像检索等复杂任务上表现不足。

Method: （1）提出自动化的细粒度多模态五元组数据构建流水线，生成包含细粒度图像标题和修改文本的CIR数据集；（2）提出两阶段细粒度微调策略，将微调过程解耦为两个阶段：细粒度上下文推理导向微调和细粒度检索导向微调，分别增强模型的上下文理解能力和查询-目标对齐能力。

Result: 在涵盖多种复杂图像检索任务的五个数据集上进行的大量实验表明，该方法在零样本检索设置下显著优于现有方法，即便使用更轻量级的MLLM骨干网络也能取得优越性能。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度上下文建模和解耦的两阶段微调策略，可以有效提升MLLMs在复杂图像检索任务中的性能，验证了该方法在零样本设定下的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Due to their strong generalizable multimodal processing and reasoning capabilities, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated significant potential as universal image retrievers, effectively addressing diverse real-world image retrieval tasks. Nevertheless, pioneering studies, while promising, overlook the potential of fine-grained context modeling and disentangled fine-tuning objectives in enhancing MLLMs' retrieval performance, particularly for complex tasks such as long-text-to-image retrieval, visual dialog retrieval, and composed image retrieval (CIR). Therefore, in this work, we propose an automated fine-grained multimodal quintuple dataset construction pipeline and a novel two-stage fine-grained multimodal fine-tuning strategy. The dataset generation pipeline produces a comprehensive CIR dataset with fine-grained image captions and modification text, facilitating fine-grained context modeling. Beyond the previously entangled fine-tuning paradigm, our approach separates the fine-tuning process into two distinct stages: (1) fine-grained context reasoning-oriented fine-tuning and (2) fine-grained retrieval-oriented fine-tuning. These stages aim to sequentially enhance the model's context understanding and query-target alignment capabilities, thereby improving retrieval performance. Extensive experiments across five datasets encompassing diverse and complex image retrieval tasks demonstrate the remarkable superiority of our method over existing approaches in zero-shot retrieval settings, even with a more lightweight MLLM backbone compared to those methods.

</details>


### [49] [FootprintNet: State-Transition-Guided Dynamic Footprint Learning for Multi-temporal Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27969)
*Haotian Zhang,Hao Chen,Han Guo,Zhengxia Zou,Zhenwei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Urban Building Dynamics Detection (UBDD)任务及FootprintNet网络，用于从多时相遥感影像中检测建筑物变化的动态足迹（即变化发生的时间区间），并通过独立的多变化类别统一建模单次与多次变化过程，配合状态-动作转移约束和边界线索增强特征对比，在多个数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感多时相变化检测方法大多假设每个空间位置在整个观测周期内仅发生一次变化，无法有效刻画与人类活动密切相关的重复变化区域。这种隐含的单变化假设限制了模型对复杂动态过程的理解能力。

Method: 提出UBDD任务定义建筑物变化动态足迹；FootprintNet将建筑物变化过程抽象为潜在状态与动作之间的交互，施加状态-动作转移约束以学习因果一致的变化轨迹；利用时间变化边界线索增强边界两侧特征对比；提出BCDS评估指标用于衡量预测足迹与标签的语义保持和时间偏移。

Result: 在TSCD、MUDS和WUSU三个数据集上的大量实验表明，FootprintNet优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入UBDD任务、FootprintNet架构和BCDS评估指标，实现了对建筑物重复变化动态足迹的精确检测，为多时相遥感变化检测提供了更精细的建模范式。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in remote sensing multi-temporal change detection (MTCD), most existing MTCD methods still represent the dynamic process at each spatial location over the entire observation period using a single change category associated with the final observation. This implicit single-change assumption limits their ability to characterize regions of recurrent change closely related to human activities. To address this limitation, we introduce Urban Building Dynamics Detection (UBDD), which identifies building-change dynamic footprints, i.e., the temporal intervals in which changes occur, from multi-temporal imagery and produces pixel-wise classification masks. For regions undergoing two or more changes, UBDD introduces an independent multi-change class for unified representation, thereby enabling unified modeling of single- and multi-change processes. Furthermore, we propose FootprintNet, which abstracts building-change processes as interactions between latent states and actions, and imposes state-action transition constraints to guide the learning of causally coherent change trajectories. It further exploits temporal change-boundary cues to enhance feature contrast across boundary sides, thereby improving the discrimination among different dynamic footprints and enabling accurate detection of dynamic footprints. Moreover, we introduce the Building Change Dynamics Score (BCDS) to address the inability of conventional metrics to reflect the temporal proximity between predicted footprints and labels. It evaluates predictions according to their preservation of change semantics and temporal offsets from the corresponding labels. Extensive experiments on TSCD, MUDS, and WUSU demonstrate that FootprintNet outperforms current state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/zmoka-zht/FootprintNet.

</details>


### [50] [Now You Have My Healthy Attention: A U-DiT for Brain-MRI Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27974)
*Danilo Danese,Angela Lombardi,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对ASNR-MICCAI BraTS局部合成（Inpainting）任务，提出一种基于U-DiT架构的确定性回归模型用于脑部MRI的解剖学合理修复。核心创新在于两点：一是约束注意力机制使被遮挡tokens仅关注同一体积中已知健康tokens，并对对侧同源（contralateral homologue）施加学习偏置；二是引入对侧对称输入作为患者特异性先验，利用大脑的近似双侧对称性提升修复质量。在BraTS-2026验证集上达到SSIM 0.864、PSNR 24.7dB、MSE 4.6×10⁻³。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤图像修复需要生成不包含病灶的健康组织解剖参考，用于下游分析。由于任务采用失真指标（SSIM、PSNR、MSE）评估，作者构建确定性回归模型，核心动机是通过有针对性的归纳偏置（inductive bias）提升修复的解剖合理性。

Method: 采用U-DiT架构：体素编码器-解码器通过下采样的全局自注意力块（带三维旋转位置编码）导入长程上下文，卷积和跳跃连接保留高频细节。两个关键创新：(1) 注意力约束——被遮挡（void）tokens仅关注同一体积中的已知健康tokens，并对各查询的对侧同源区域施加学习偏置，确保修复信息来自观察到的解剖结构而非未知区域；(2) 对侧对称输入——提供镜像的健康半球作为患者特异性先验，利用大脑近似双侧对称且病变通常单侧的特点。

Result: 在官方BraTS-2026验证排行榜上，该提交在219例数据上达到平均健康区域SSIM为0.864、PSNR为24.7dB、MSE为4.6×10⁻³的结果。

Conclusion: 作者进一步分析了失真最优回归固有的残差平滑性问题，并讨论了其对解剖真实性的影响，暗示纯基于失真指标的回归模型在解剖细节保真方面存在局限。

Abstract: The ASNR-MICCAI BraTS Local Synthesis (Inpainting) task asks for the anatomically plausible completion of healthy brain tissue within a masked region of a T1-weighted MRI, providing a tumor-free anatomical reference for downstream analysis. As the task is scored by distortion metrics (SSIM, PSNR, MSE), we build a deterministic regression model and focus on giving it inductive biases tailored to inpainting. Our network follows the U-DiT principle of performing self-attention on a downsampled token grid: a volumetric encoder-decoder imports long-range context through a downsampled global self-attention block with three-dimensional rotary position embeddings, while convolutions and skip connections preserve high-frequency detail. Two ideas drive our results. First, we constrain the attention so that occluded ("void") tokens attend only to known-healthy tokens of the same volume, with a learned bias toward each query's contralateral homologue, forcing the completion to be inferred from observed anatomy rather than from other unknown regions. Second, we add a contralateral-symmetry input that supplies the mirrored healthy hemisphere as a patient-specific prior; since the brain is approximately bilaterally symmetric and lesions are typically unilateral, this prior improves the distortion metrics at matched structural similarity. On the official BraTS-2026 validation leaderboard our submission reaches a mean healthy-region SSIM of $0.864$, PSNR of $24.7$\,dB and MSE of $4.6{\times}10^{-3}$ over $219$ cases. We further analyse the residual smoothness inherent to distortion-optimal regression and discuss its implications for anatomical realism.

</details>


### [51] [ViP-Rig: Visual-Prompted Controllable Rigging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27982)
*Zihan Qin,Mingze Sun,Yifan Mao,Jialei Xu,Jingfeng Guo,Changrong Hu,Wenbo Zhao,Junjun Jiang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViP-Rig是一个视觉提示驱动的绑定框架，通过注入用户绘制或编辑的2D骨骼和刚性提示特征到冻结的预训练骨干网络中，实现提示优先绑定和结果引导编辑，在骨架生成和蒙皮预测两个阶段均优于几何条件基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绑定本质上是任务相关的，同一网格在不同动画任务中需要不同的骨架和形变行为，而现有自动方法仅从几何生成貌似合理的绑定，对骨架和形变行为缺乏显式控制。艺术家需要反复检查和编辑初始绑定，因此需要一种支持用户显式控制的方法。

Method: ViP-Rig包含两个阶段：(1)骨架生成阶段，利用Dense-to-Compact视觉提示编码将手绘骨架草图处理为紧凑的固定长度条件token，通过门控适配器注入冻结的预训练自回归生成器，控制关节点位置和分支结构；(2)蒙皮预测阶段，用相同的视觉编码设计处理刚性图，将结果token对称注入点流和关节流以调节点-关节兼容性及蒙皮权重，同时保持预训练蒙皮骨干冻结。

Result: 在Articulation-XL2.0数据集和ModelsResource零样本评估上，ViP-Rig在提示引导评估下比几何条件基线更准确地恢复目标骨架和蒙皮权重。定性结果展示了在提示优先绑定和结果引导编辑中显式且局部的控制能力。

Conclusion: ViP-Rig通过视觉提示注入冻结预训练骨干的方式，实现了对绑定过程的显式用户控制，同时保留了预训练模型的几何先验，为任务相关的绑定生成提供了有效的交互式解决方案。

Abstract: Rigging is inherently task-dependent because the same mesh may require different skeletons and deformation behaviors across animation tasks. In practice, artists often inspect an initial rig and repeatedly edit its skeletal structure and deformation behavior to meet specific animation requirements. Existing automatic methods primarily generate a plausible rig from geometry, offering limited explicit control over the resulting skeleton and deformation behavior. In this work, we present ViP-Rig, a visual-prompted framework that supports both prompt-first rigging and result-guided editing by injecting features extracted from user-drawn or edited 2D skeletal and rigidity prompts into frozen pretrained backbones. Specifically, ViP-Rig consists of two stages, Skeleton Generation and Skinning Prediction. In the first stage, the skeletal sketch is processed by the Dense-to-Compact Visual Prompt Encoding to produce compact, fixed-length conditioning tokens. The resulting tokens are injected into a frozen pretrained autoregressive generator through gated adapters to control joint placement and branching structure while preserving the generator's geometric prior. In the second stage, the rigidity map is processed using the same visual encoding design, while the pretrained skinning backbone remains frozen. The resulting tokens are symmetrically injected into the point and joint streams to modulate point-joint compatibility and the resulting skinning weights. Experiments on Articulation-XL2.0 and zero-shot evaluation on ModelsResource show that ViP-Rig more accurately recovers target skeletons and skinning weights than geometry-conditioned baselines under prompt-guided evaluation. Qualitative results further demonstrate explicit and localized control in both prompt-first rigging and result-guided editing.

</details>

Abstract: Declining insect populations make reliable biodiversity monitoring increasingly urgent, yet monitoring of insect biodiversity is hampered by a lack of standardised data and by costly and time-consuming manual identification by expert entomologists. Our model improves on non-hierarchical classifiers by leveraging biological taxonomy to extract granularity-specific visual features and makes hierarchy-consistent predictions to the deepest taxonomic level that meets a confidence threshold (T = 0.6). Our model achieved a per-level accuracy of 80-99% on test data, across five levels of hierarchy. Furthermore ...

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (FMs) are models trained on vast amounts of typically unlabeled data and have been shown to yield regularized latent spaces that can be used effectively in downstream classification tasks. This is also true for the classification of mitotic figures vs. other cells. In this work, we investigate this question for common current pathology FMs (UNI, UNI2-h, Virchow, Virchow2, H-optimus-0, H-optimus-1) and compare their performance against a fully end-to-end trained baseline based on a ResNet50 architecture. We combine FM backbones with representatives of single stage, dual stage and self-attention-based detectors (RetinaNet, Faster R-CNN, Deformable DETR respectively) on the multi-domain MIDOG++ dataset, and on the TUPAC16 dataset as an out-of-domain case. We show that the H-optimus-0 and Virchow models yielded competitive performance, indicating that the latent spaces of current FMs, all trained on image-level self-supervision, are suitable for direct mitotic figure detection and may be slightly more robust on our out-of-domain test case. All code is made available publicly at https://github.com/DeepMicroscopy/FM4MFdet.

### [54] [ENCORE: Event-Assisted Complementary Motion Refinement for Learned Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28020)
*Shuhan Ye,Hongbin Yu,Chenqi Kong,Pingchuan Ma,Chong Wang,Jun Wan,Qixin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ENCORE框架，利用事件相机辅助的互补运动细化来提升学习型视频压缩的性能。事件作为辅助模态，通过与RGB特征分解为公共与模态特有表示，并结合能量校准与路由机制，仅细化RGB运动估计而不改变编码目标，在多个数据集上实现了显著的BD-rate节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型视频压缩依赖离散采样的RGB帧进行运动估计，在快速运动、模糊、遮挡、弱纹理、低光照和亮度突变等场景下运动估计不准确。事件相机能异步捕捉RGB时间戳之间的细粒度强度变化，为帧间动态提供互补证据，但尚未被充分利用于视频压缩中的运动建模。

Method: ENCORE包含三个核心组件：(1) 互补运动表示(CMR)将对齐的RGB-事件特征分解为公共运动表示和模态特有运动表示；(2) 空间能量与冗余知情校准(SERIC)识别事件特有且相对于RGB活跃的响应，抑制弱或冗余证据，预测候选光流修正；(3) 能量感知路由(EAR)决定修正应在何处以及以何种强度细化RGB光流。事件仅作为运动建模的辅助模态，RGB仍是唯一的编码和重建目标。

Result: 在BS-ERGB、HQ-EVFI和CED三个数据集上，ENCORE在不同GOP长度下均取得一致提升。在BS-ERGB上，PSNR-RGB BD-rate节省最高达20.80%，MS-SSIM-RGB BD-rate节省最高达22.14%，其余两个数据集上也保持明显改进。

Conclusion: 事件模态能够有效地为学习型视频压缩提供互补的运动信息，通过精心设计的分解-校准-路由机制，可以在不改变编码与重建目标的前提下显著提升运动估计精度和压缩效率。

Abstract: Learned video compression relies on accurate temporal modeling to remove redundancy between adjacent frames. However, most existing codecs infer motion solely from discretely sampled RGB frames, making their estimates vulnerable to fast motion, blur, occlusion, weak texture, low illumination, and abrupt brightness changes. Event cameras asynchronously capture fine-grained intensity changes between RGB timestamps and therefore provide complementary evidence about inter-frame dynamics. We propose ENCORE, an Event-Assisted Complementary Motion Refinement framework for learned video compression. ENCORE first employs Complementary Motion Representation (CMR) to decompose aligned RGB-event features into common and modality-specific motion representations. Spatial Energy and Redundancy-Informed Calibration (SERIC) then identifies event-specific responses that are active and novel relative to RGB, suppresses weak or redundant evidence, and predicts a candidate flow correction. Finally, Energy-Aware Routing (EAR) determines where and how strongly the correction should refine the RGB flow. Events serve solely as an auxiliary modality for motion modeling, while RGB remains the only coding and reconstruction target. Experiments on BS-ERGB, HQ-EVFI, and CED demonstrate consistent gains across datasets and GOP lengths. On BS-ERGB, ENCORE achieves up to 20.80% PSNR-RGB and 22.14% MS-SSIM-RGB BD-rate savings, while retaining clear improvements on the other two datasets.

</details>


### [55] [Split and Drive: Dual-Axis Disentanglement for Real-Time Gaussian Head Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28032)
*MD Wahiduzzaman Khan,Mingshan Jia,Xiaolin Zhang,En Yu,Kaska Musial-Gabrys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpiD（Split and Drive）是一个基于单张图像构建的3D高斯头部头像框架，通过"计算轴"和"特征轴"两个解耦维度实现：计算轴将每帧驱动内化，无需外部跟踪管线；特征轴将头像分解为三个专门的高斯分支，分别建模几何上不同的面部区域。实验表明该方法在保持SOTA性能的同时，实现了所有对比方法中最快的推理速度（包含完整驱动流程）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D高斯头像方法（1）依赖外部跟踪管线，其延迟被排除在推理测量之外，实际端到端性能被高估；（2）使用统一表示编码几何上截然不同的面部区域（如眼睛、口部、皮肤），限制了表达能力和渲染保真度。

Method: SpiD基于两个解耦轴设计：（1）计算轴——将每帧的驱动计算内化到网络本身，消除对外部跟踪管线的依赖；（2）特征轴——将头像分解为三个专门的高斯分支，每个分支独立建模一个几何上不同的面部域（如眼部、口部、面部其余区域），从而避免统一表示带来的纠缠问题。

Result: 在广泛实验中，SpiD在保持与SOTA方法相当甚至更强的性能的同时，在单个GPU上完整驱动管线（含所有处理步骤）实现了所有对比方法中最快的推理速度。

Conclusion: 通过计算轴与特征轴的双重解耦，SpiD证明可以在无需外部跟踪的前提下实现高效、高保真的单图像可驱动头像合成，为数字人物合成领域提供了更实用的实时方案。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic animatable head avatars from a single image remains a fundamental challenge in digital human synthesis. While recent 3D Gaussian Splatting methods have achieved promising results, they rely on external tracking pipelines whose latency is excluded from inference measurements. Furthermore, they adopt unified representations that entangle geometrically distinct facial regions, limiting both expressiveness and rendering fidelity. We propose SpiD (Split and Drive), a single-image Gaussian head avatar framework built on two disentanglement axes. The compute axis internalizes per-frame driving, eliminating external tracking dependency at inference. The feature axis decomposes the avatar into three specialized Gaussian branches, each modeling a geometrically distinct facial domain. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistently strong performance against state-of-the-art methods while achieving the fastest inference speed among all compared methods on a single GPU with the complete driving pipeline included.

</details>


### [56] [TongueReenact: Geometry-Anchored Tongue Synthesis for Face Reenactment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28039)
*MD Wahiduzzaman Khan,Mingshan Jia,Xiaolin Zhang,En Yu,Kaska Musial-Gabrys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个跨身份舌部动态迁移的人脸再现框架，通过基础模型辅助引导的引导生产线生成专用舌头分割模型，并利用空间约束的潜在掩码扩散模型实现逼真的舌头合成，在舌头特异性指标上比所有基线提升两倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸再现系统虽然能实现令人印象深刻的姿态和表情迁移，但大多忽略舌部动态，导致说话和表情动作时口腔内部在解剖学上不一致。作者旨在解决这一被忽视的关键问题，实现逼真的舌部动态迁移。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) 基础模型辅助的引导式生产线（bootstrapping pipeline），无需人工标注即可生成面向野外再现场景的专用舌头分割模型；(2) 空间约束的潜在掩码扩散模型（spatially constrained latent masked diffusion model），用于逼真舌头合成，并采用自适应掩码扩张实现口腔边界的无缝过渡。

Result: 大量实验表明，在每项舌头特异性指标上，该方法比所有基线提升了两倍以上。此外，提出的基于VLM的评估协议能够在规模上复现专家标注，并在所有消融变体上确认了感知优越性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次解决了人脸再现中的跨身份舌部动态迁移问题，通过创新性的训练管线和扩散模型架构，实现了解剖学一致且感知上优越的舌部合成效果，并提供了可扩展的自动化评估方案。

Abstract: Modern face reenactment systems achieve impressive pose and expression transfer using geometry-driven representations. However, they largely ignore tongue dynamics, leading to anatomically inconsistent mouth interiors during speech and expressive motions. We introduce the first framework for cross-identity tongue dynamics transfer in face reenactment. We propose a foundation-model-assisted bootstrapping pipeline that produces a dedicated tongue segmentation model for in-the-wild reenactment without curated annotations. We further introduce a spatially constrained latent masked diffusion model for realistic tongue synthesis, with adaptive mask dilation for seamless mouth boundary transitions. Extensive experiments demonstrate improvements of more than two times over all baselines on every tongue-specific metric. We additionally propose a VLM-based evaluation protocol that replicates expert annotation at scale, confirming perceptual superiority across all ablation variants.

</details>


### [57] [Temporal Concentration from Rollout Errors: Implicit Preference Optimization for Text-to-Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28058)
*Henglin Liu,Fangyuan Kong,Jing Wang,Yizhou Lin,Nisha Huang,Chang Liu,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: cIPO是一个面向视频扩散模型的后训练框架，通过从去噪过程中提取隐式偏好信号，并对时间高误差片段进行集中优化，有效解决了视频生成中运动坍缩、物体闪烁等时间稀疏伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DPO的视频偏好对齐方法虽提升了视觉质量，但面临时间稀疏伪影（如运动坍缩、物体闪烁、颜色过饱和）的挑战。根本原因在于两个瓶颈：(1)偏好归因瓶颈——离线人工标注成本高且无法准确捕捉学习动态，在线奖励信号虽能感知rollout但不稳定且有偏；(2)时间信用错配——统一监督无法精准定位伪影出现的短暂片段。

Method: cIPO从去噪过程中直接推导隐式偏好信号：给定真实视频，模型添加前向噪声后通过迭代去噪重建，将原始视频视为偏好样本，重建结果视为非偏好样本。这一设计无需人工标注或外部奖励模型即可捕获推理时误差。此外，cIPO计算原始与重建视频之间的时间重建误差，将优化集中在高误差片段上，实现失败易发区域的精准修正。

Result: 大量实验表明，cIPO在多个数据集上持续提升了视频真实性和时间一致性，验证了隐式偏好结合时间集中优化的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: cIPO通过隐式偏好采样和时间集中优化，有效解决了视频生成中时间稀疏伪影问题，为视频扩散模型的后训练偏好对齐提供了一种无需外部标注或奖励模型的高效方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in preference alignment for diffusion-based video generation, particularly via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), have significantly improved visual quality. However, temporally sparse artifacts such as motion collapse, object flickering, and color oversaturation remain a major barrier to perceptual realism. Existing methods struggle with these issues due to two key limitations: (1) the preference attribution bottleneck, where offline human annotations are costly and fail to accurately capture learning dynamics, while online reward signals are rollout-aware but often unstable and biased; and (2) temporal credit misallocation, where uniformly applied supervision cannot effectively target the brief segments in which artifacts occur. To address these challenges, we propose concentrated Implicit Preference Optimization (cIPO), a post-training framework for video diffusion models. cIPO derives implicit preference signals directly from the denoising process: given a real video, the model adds forward noise and reconstructs it via iterative denoising, treating the original as the preferred sample and the reconstruction as the dispreferred one. This formulation captures inference-time errors without requiring human annotations or external reward models. Moreover, frame-level discrepancies between original and reconstructed videos reveal when failures occur. cIPO leverages this by computing temporal reconstruction errors and concentrating optimization on high-error segments, enabling more precise correction of failure-prone regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that cIPO consistently enhances video authenticity and temporal coherence across multiple datasets, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of implicit preference with temporally concentrated optimization.

</details>


### [58] [Landmark shape spaces with induced metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28064)
*Sarang Joshi,Peter W. Michor,Stefan Sommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文统一了Kendall的标志点形状空间（去除刚体运动并固定尺度）与带右不变Sobolev度量的微分同胚群下降标志点配置空间，通过引入"筛选弹性算子"（screened elasticity operator）构造新的标志点形状空间，同时具备两种方法的优点：防止标志点碰撞、度量在环境空间中定义且与标志点数量无关、保持局部刚体变换、去除全局刚体运动并固定尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kendall形状空间和基于右不变Sobolev度量的标志点配置空间各有优势但互不相容：Kendall空间通过模去刚体运动实现形状不变性，但缺乏正则性保护（标志点可能碰撞）；Sobolev度量空间能防止碰撞且具有环境空间定义的正则度量，但未同时实现全局刚体运动去除和尺度固定。作者希望融合两者，获得兼具正则性和形状不变性的统一框架。

Method: 作者定义了"筛选弹性算子"（screened elasticity operator），其零空间恰好由刚体运动张成；证明了该算子可以下降（descend）到商空间以得到所需几何结构；并提出了数值求解匹配问题和计算测地线的方案。

Result: 构造出的新标志点形状空间具备以下关键性质：下降度量的正则性防止标志点碰撞；度量在环境空间中定义，独立于标志点数量；保留局部刚体变换；去除全局刚体运动；固定尺度。该构造允许在应用中使用的标志点配置空间具有足够正则的度量，同时保留Kendall形状空间的标志性形状不变性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架将Kendall形状空间与微分同胚群下降的Sobolev度量方法有机结合，生成兼具正则性和形状不变性的形状空间，为形状分析应用提供了理论上一致且数值可行的新工具。

Abstract: We present a unification of Kendall's landmark shape spaces, where rigid motions are factored out and scale fixed on landmark configurations equipped with Euclidean geometry, with landmark configuration spaces carrying Riemannian metrics descending from right-invariant Sobolev metrics on the diffeomorphism group. The resulting new landmark shape spaces achieve the defining properties of both approaches: The regularity of the descending metric prevents landmarks from colliding, the metric is defined in the ambient space independent of the number of landmarks, local rigid transformations are preserved, global rigid motions are removed, and scale fixed. To achieve this, we define a particular Sobolev-type operator, the screened elasticity operator, whose null-space consists exactly of the rigid motions, we show how this operator descends to achieve the desired geometry, and we present approaches to solving matching problems and computing geodesics numerically. The resulting construction allows the use of landmark configuration spaces with sufficiently regular metrics in applications while retaining the shape invariances that are a hallmark of Kendall's shape spaces.

</details>


### [59] [BladeYOLO: Wind Turbine Blade Defect Detection with Limited Annotations and Weak-Saliency Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28065)
*Yabin Xu,Fangtao Zhang,Fan Wang,Zhan Wang,Honghua Chen,Mingqiang Wei,Haoran Xie,Sam Kwong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BladeYOLO，一种风电机组叶片缺陷检测框架，通过将DINOv3自监督预训练的ViT骨干集成到YOLOv12-L中，并结合Mamba引导的弱缺陷增强模块和轻量级风格注入模块，有效解决了实际巡检场景中缺陷小尺度、低对比度、标注数据有限等挑战。实验表明该方法在WTBlade-Defect数据集上表现优异，在公开Wind Surface Defect数据集上比最佳对比方法mAP50提升3.5%，mAP50-95提升2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电机组叶片缺陷检测在实际巡检中面临两大核心挑战：一是现场标注数据稀缺，限制了大模型的有效训练；二是叶片缺陷通常具有小尺度、低对比度且与复杂背景难以区分的视觉特征，现有检测器的鲁棒性不足。

Method: BladeYOLO框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将DINOv3自监督预训练的Vision Transformer骨干集成到YOLOv12-L中，实现大规模通用视觉先验的迁移； (2) Mamba引导的弱缺陷增强模块，包含保留高频结构线索的细节增强多尺度分支和将高层语义渐进传播到浅层特征的Cross-Mamba模块； (3) 轻量级风格注入模块，通过傅里叶分解捕获环境相关风格信息并注入到选定的ViT自注意力层中，提升对外观变化的鲁棒性。

Result: 在WTBlade-Defect数据集上BladeYOLO取得了优越性能；在更少的标注预算下仍能保持良好的检测效果；在公开Wind Surface Defect数据集上，BladeYOLO分别比最佳对比方法在mAP50和mAP50-95上高出3.5%和2.5%，验证了跨数据集鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BladeYOLO通过自监督预训练特征迁移、Mamba引导的弱缺陷增强以及风格注入的联合设计，有效解决了有限标注和弱视觉特征下的叶片缺陷检测问题，同时展现出良好的跨数据集泛化能力，为实际风电场巡检中的缺陷检测提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Wind turbine blade defect detection remains highly challenging in real-world inspection scenarios due to limited on-site data and the subtle visual characteristics of defects. In practice, blade defects are often small-scale, low-contrast, and difficult to distinguish from complex backgrounds, which significantly limits the robustness of existing detectors. To address these challenges, we propose BladeYOLO, a defect detection framework for wind turbine blades. Specifically, we integrate a Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone initialized with DINOv3 self-supervised pre-trained weights into YOLOv12-L, enabling the transfer of large-scale generic visual priors to blade defect detection and improving feature representation under limited training annotations. To enhance the perception of subtle defects, we further develop a Mamba-guided Weak-Defect Enhancement module, which consists of a Detail-Enhanced Multi-scale Branch for preserving high-frequency structural cues and a Cross-Mamba module for progressively propagating high-level semantic guidance to shallow features. In addition, we introduce a lightweight Style-Injector module that captures environment-related style information via Fourier decomposition and injects it into selected ViT self-attention layers, thereby improving robustness against environment-induced appearance variations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BladeYOLO achieves superior performance on the WTBlade-Defect dataset, with additional annotation-budget experiments showing its favorable performance under reduced training annotations. Evaluation on the public Wind Surface Defect dataset further provides supportive evidence for the cross-dataset robustness of BladeYOLO. In particular, on this public dataset, BladeYOLO outperforms the best competing method by 3.5\% in mAP$_{50}$ and 2.5\% in mAP$_{50-95}$.

</details>


### [60] [mmRadarTwin: A Measurement-Calibrated Signal-Level Digital Twin Platform for Indoor mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28108)
*Jianyi Zhou,Chenghao Zhang,Yanli Li,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: mmRadarTwin 是一个面向室内毫米波雷达的信号级、路径属性数字孪生平台，通过共享接收通道与距离-角度处理接口连接真实雷达测量分支和 Unreal Engine 场景仿真分支，能够生成复数多通道接收网格并导出逐路径贡献记录，用于雷达数字孪生的构建、比较与诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内毫米波雷达感知难以复现，因为实测距离-角度响应依赖于场景几何、材料响应、多径、硬件约定和信号处理等多种因素。现有光线追踪和数字孪生工具通常只暴露渲染、信道或路径级量，而雷达感知需要能够与真实 FMCW 测量在同一域中处理和比较的复杂信号产品。

Method: mmRadarTwin 将真实雷达测量分支与 Unreal Engine 场景仿真分支通过共享的接收通道和距离-角度处理接口连接起来。仿真器写出复数多通道接收网格，并导出逐路径贡献记录，每条记录标识参与者（actor）、材料标签、传播事件以及每条仿真回波的输出 bin 支持。评估使用商用单基地毫米波雷达和移动场景采集硬件在办公场景中部署。

Result: 在覆盖 22 个雷达位置、154 个测量姿态的评估中，当前纯物理路径基仿真器在中心可用视场内对测量活跃的几何支持响应区域召回率达 70.8%，同时暴露了由弱或缺失路径支持、响应偏移、不支持的锚点和缺失物理机制导致的残差。

Conclusion: mmRadarTwin 不声称实现完整的雷达地图重建或跨房间泛化，而是建立了一个实用的系统工作流程，用于构建、比较和诊断室内雷达数字孪生。

Abstract: Indoor mmWave radar perception is difficult to reproduce because measured range-angle responses depend on scene geometry, material response, multipath, hardware conventions, and signal processing. Existing ray-tracing and digital-twin tools often expose rendering, channel, or path-level quantities, while radar sensing requires complex signal products that can be processed and compared in the same domain as real FMCW measurements. We present mmRadarTwin, a signal-level and path-attributed digital-twin platform for indoor mmWave radar. mmRadarTwin links a real radar measurement branch with an Unreal Engine scene-simulation branch through a shared receive-channel and range-angle processing interface. The simulator writes complex multi-channel receive grids and exports per-path contribution records that identify the actor, material tag, propagation event, and output-bin support of each simulated return. We evaluate mmRadarTwin in an office deployment using a commodity monostatic mmWave radar and mobile scene-capture hardware. Across 154 measured poses spanning 22 radar locations, the current physics-only path-basis simulator recalls 70.8% of measurement-active geometry-supported response regions in the central usable field of view while exposing residuals caused by weak or missing path support, shifted responses, unsupported anchors, and missing physical mechanisms. Rather than claiming complete radar-map reconstruction or cross-room generalization, mmRadarTwin establishes a practical systems workflow for constructing, comparing, and diagnosing indoor radar digital twins.

</details>


### [61] [Towards Practical Algorithm Selection for Unsupervised Domain Adaptation in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28125)
*Yiheng Xiong,Luisa Gallée,Daniel Santak Wolf,Heiko Hillenhagen,Michael Götz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需标签的模型选择准则，用于在无监督域适应（UDA）中同时选择最优算法和超参数。通过两级协议参考构建，在无目标标签的情况下对候选模型打分排序，并在多个医学影像数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中存在大量UDA算法，但由于目标域无标签，难以直接评估模型性能，导致算法和超参数的选择不明确，缺乏有效的标签免费选择机制。

Method: 提出两级协议参考（agreement reference）构建方法：第一级利用多个无标签选择信号，在每个算法内提名一个候选模型；第二级跨算法聚合被提名模型，形成每个无标签目标样本的参考预测。最终选择与参考预测协议度最高的候选模型进行部署。

Result: 在四个脑部MRI和四个胸部X光数据集上，覆盖七个临床相关迁移场景的实验表明，该方法在选择性能上优于其他方法，且在不同算法池下均保持有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为实现临床部署中无标签的UDA算法选择迈出了实际可行的一步，有助于在实践中更可靠地选用合适的UDA算法和超参数。

Abstract: Numerous unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) algori-thms exist, but for clinical practice, selecting the best-suited one along with proper hyperparameters often remains unclear, as the unlabeled deployment (target) domain prevents direct evaluation. We propose a label-free criterion that jointly selects the algorithm and hyperparameters for UDA. Given a pool of candidate models from multiple algorithms trained with different hyperparameters, our approach scores each candidate against an agreement reference, and selects the one with the highest score. The agreement reference is constructed in two levels without using target labels. First, we leverage multiple label-free selection signals, using each to nominate a model within every algorithm. Second, the nominated models are aggregated across algorithms to form a reference prediction for each unlabeled target sample. The candidate whose predictions agree most with this reference is then selected for deployment. Experimental results on four brain MRI and four chest X-ray datasets across seven clinically relevant transfer scenarios show that our method achieves better selection performance than other methods and remains effective across different algorithm pools. Our approach takes a step towards practical, label-free algorithm selection for clinical deployment of UDA.

</details>


### [62] [Face and Voice Cross-modal Association with Learning Convex Feature Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28129)
*Taewan Kim,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单的跨模态特征嵌入方法用于人脸与语音的关联学习，通过在跨模态特征之间制造另一个特征，使得同一人的声纹和面部特征嵌入凸包中，并结合跨模态注意力机制减少类间差异，在VoxCeleb数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨模态关联方法虽然进行了跨模态判别，但低估了音频和视频模态间特征异质性问题的重要性，导致大量假阳性和假阴性结果。作者旨在解决跨模态特征之间的异质性问题，以提升人脸与语音关联的准确性。

Method: 提出一种跨模态特征嵌入方法，核心思路是在跨模态特征之间生成另一个中间特征，使同一人的语音特征和面部特征被嵌入到一个凸包（convex hull）中。同时引入跨模态注意力机制与凸嵌入技术相结合，通过最小化类间差异来减少假阳性和假阴性。

Result: 在大型VoxCeleb数据集上对跨模态验证、匹配和检索任务进行了全面评估，实验结果表明该方法相比现有的最先进方法取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的凸嵌入与跨模态注意力结合的方法能够有效缓解跨模态特征的异质性问题，降低假阳性和假阴性，在跨模态人脸-语音关联任务中展现出了优越的性能。

Abstract: Face-and-voice association learning is one of the most challenging tasks in deep learning. In this paper, we propose a simple but powerful cross-modal feature embedding method for the association of faces and voices. Previous work has studied cross-modal association tasks to establish the correlation between voice clips and facial images. These works have addressed cross-modal discrimination but underestimate the importance of handling heterogeneity in inter-modal features between audio and video, resulting in a lot of false positives and false negatives. To tackle the problem, the proposed method learns the embeddings of cross-modal features by making another feature exist between cross-modal features, facilitating the voice and face features of the same person to be embedded in a convex hull. Moreover, the incorporation of cross-modal attention mechanisms with convex embedding techniques represents a highly effective strategy for the attenuation of false positives and false negatives, accomplished via the minimization of inter-class discrepancies. We exhaustively evaluated our method for cross-modal verification, matching, and retrieval tasks on the large-scale VoxCeleb dataset. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves notable improvements over existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [63] [VAD: Attributing Visual Evidence for Target Reconstruction in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28590)
*Kangning Zhang,Yixing Li,Shuai Shao,Qingyao Li,Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Jianghao Lin,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉归因蒸馏（VAD），一种反事实目标重建算法，通过估计教师修正中由视觉证据支持的部分来改善多模态在线蒸馏，在6个细粒度视觉基准上超越直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态在线蒸馏（OPD）通过特权视图教师监督学生生成轨迹来迁移细粒度视觉知识，但其下一令牌修正是源混合的，将视觉信号、语言先验和教师特定效应混在一起。现有方法的挑战在于估计哪些修正是由视觉证据支持的，而不仅仅是何处或如何强烈地进行蒸馏。

Method: VAD在每个学生生成的前缀处，使用相同的固定教师分别在有证据和无证据（移除）条件下评估，通过中心化对数概率的差异定义ut（视觉证据方向的带符号代理），估计证据支持或反驳候选令牌的程度。VAD将原始修正投影到该代理上，获得干预对齐分量和代理未解释残差，然后从前者重建学生锚定的目标。训练中，重建目标提供主要监督信号，特权教师仅提供弱正则化。

Result: 在4B和9B规模的六个细粒度视觉基准上，VAD优于直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权。令牌级别和受控目标分析表明，代理对齐分量富含任务相关的视觉修正，并产生更强的目标偏移，尤其是当证据反驳错误答案时。

Conclusion: 反事实目标重建是源混合监督的有效替代方案，通过分离视觉可归因的修正成分来提升多模态蒸馏的效果。

Abstract: Multimodal on-policy distillation (OPD) transfers fine-grained visual knowledge by supervising student-generated trajectories with a privileged-view teacher. Yet its next-token corrections are source-mixed, combining visual signals with linguistic priors and teacher-specific effects. The key challenge is to estimate which corrections are supported by visual evidence, not merely where or how strongly to distill. We introduce Visual Attribution Distillation (VAD), a counterfactual target-reconstruction algorithm that estimates the visually attributable part of a teacher correction. At each student-generated prefix, VAD evaluates the same fixed teacher with the relevant evidence present and removed. The corresponding change in centered log-probabilities defines ut, a signed proxy for the visual evidence direction that estimates how revealing the evidence supports or refutes candidate tokens. VAD projects the original correction onto this proxy to obtain an intervention-aligned component and a proxy-unexplained residual, then reconstructs a student-anchored target from the former. During training, this reconstructed target supplies the primary supervision signal, while the privileged teacher contributes a weak regularizer. Across six fine-grained visual benchmarks at 4B and 9B scales, VAD outperforms direct privileged-view distillation and visual-advantage weighting. Token- level and controlled-target analyses show that the proxy-aligned component is enriched in task-relevant visual corrections and yields stronger target shifts, especially when evidence refutes a mistaken answer. These results support counterfactual target reconstruction as an effective alternative to source-mixed supervision.

</details>


### [64] [Collaborative Feature Aggregation for Face Super-Resolution and Robust Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28130)
*Juheon Hwang,Taewan Kim,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Transformer的协作式人脸超分辨率和鲁棒行人重识别方法，利用多时序或多视角的关联人脸观测进行特征聚合，通过级联SR网络渐进恢复高分辨率人脸图像，并统一身份特征以提升重识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人脸超分辨率方法在恢复低质量图像中的人脸时容易产生模糊和失真；基于人脸关键点或分割的图像/视频SR方法也面临类似挑战。需要利用多个相关人脸观测（跨时间或视角）来克服低分辨率导致的身份信息丢失问题。

Method: 提出Transformer-based协作特征聚合方法，统一来自多序列或多视角数据的身份特征；设计级联SR网络渐进恢复目标人脸的HR图像，并在渐进过程中逐步统一面部特征；将统一后的身份表示应用于行人重识别任务。

Result: 大量实验和对比表明，方法在人脸超分辨率和重识别性能上均优于其他最先进方法，取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 联合身份重建与渐进式图像恢复从多个面部输入中有效提升了下游视觉识别任务性能，验证了多视角/多时序协作方法的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a novel collaborative approach for face super-resolution (SR) and robust person re-identification from sequential or multi-view facial images. Traditional SR methods often suffer from blurring and distortion in faces recovered from poor-quality images due to low resolution.
